1,291 entries, instantly-webhooks to isambard. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **instantly-webhooks**: Receive and authenticate Instantly (Instantly.ai cold email) webhooks. Use when setting up an Instantly webhook handler, securing it with the custom headers Instantly lets you attach (e.g. Authorization: Basic ... or a secret header), debugging why the header check fails, or handling campaign events like email_sent, email_opened, reply_received, email_bounced, lead_interested, lead_unsubscribed and lead_meeting_booked. Instantly does not sign webhooks: there is no signature header or HMAC. ([hookdeck/webhook-skills](https://github.com/hookdeck/webhook-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/instantly-webhooks) · ★102 · 1 skill)
